                  ABSTRACT



                   Black Mountain College was active for 24 years, from 1933 until 1957. A college, located in
                   North Carolina, based on liberal arts education, where teaching and community life were
                   combined and where experimentation, from the different knowledge areas, that formed its
                   program, was key in the educational process. A place of synergies where various disciplines
                   and interests were the heart of the artistic experience. Over its existence, a lot of leading
                   figures, who were fundamental at the American cultural and artistic scene, approached the
                  college to share their knowledge and experience. All of them contributed to creating a myth
                  around the institution. Within this context, architecture found its place in this academic project
                   in the form of professionals who were involved and

                   proposals and actions that prove that architecture can be understood as an 'event'.


                  This research study in depth the teaching and learning of architecture in this institution,
                   bringing back the architects and professionals involved. They gave shape to the academic
                   program combining theoretical knowledges with practical experience, that in its construction
                   aspect they even built some of the facilities of the campus. At the same time, the college
                  history is described through the collective multidisciplinary actions that took place and reflects
                   the ability of architecture to build, not only in space but also in time, across situations that go
                   beyond construction and can be named as 'events' .

                   PLANTING AND JUSTIFICATION OF TEMA


                   This student claims to study architecture and architecture at Black Mountain College, in conjunction with
                   BMC and college. A discipline that, although it is a priori relevant in its academic curriculum, is only
                  analyzed by its program, its actions and its results.




                   The BMC was a North American educational institution that existed between 1933 and 1957 in Black
                   Mountain, Carolina del Norte. Lo acontecido in the BMC shows one of the examples and models of
                   collective creation where the participation and collaboration between students and professors results
                   fundamentally. A place of confluence of diverse interests and specialties, in an intellectual, heuristic and
                   vital situation, which is based on the vision of other transversal disciplines in addition to architectural
                   propia, tales like science and art in general. All within the context of generalized instability in which it
                  collapsed, arose as a result of the future processes of transformation of economic, political, social and
                  cultural structures. The crack of 1929 and the Great Depression that followed, in the USA, and the
                   repercussions of other events in Europe and the height of totalitarian regimes in the different continents,
                   its background context in which the surge was instituted.




                   The BMC has been studied mainly from an artistic approximation, as an educational paradigm of
                   progressive training in the liberal arts, by the professional distinctions related to the arts that have been
                   taught by all, as well as by the repercusión of some of those who have been alumnos1 . It is a center of
                   studies and residence for students and teachers. A combination of art and community where the
                   experimentation of the various disciplines is essential in training: painting, escultura, music, dance,
                   literature, poetry, architecture and photography, without forgetting the rest of the basic and obligatory
                   assignments (history, literature, idioms, etc.). In all the discussions of the BMC arises the idea of
                  experimentation, both in the context of education, as in life in community or art.




                   The college is considered a laboratory of media and forms of expression, educational and artistic
                   innovation. An educational project based on the participatory community that was consolidated as a hub
                   for the creation of artists, thinkers, writers, professors and scientists. Some of the professionals who are
                   recognized as BMC professors are Josef and Anni Albers, Eric Bentley, Alfred Kazin, John Cage, Harry
                   Callahan, Robert Creeley, Merce Cunningham, Max Dehn, Robert Duncan, Alfred Einstein, Willem and
                  Elaine de Kooning, Richard Buckminster Fuller, Walter Gropius, Lou Harrison, Franz Kline, Jacob
                   Lawrence, Richard Lippold, Katherine Litz, Robert Motherwell, Charles Olson, Aldous Huxley, David
                   Tudor, Ben Shahn, Aaron Siskind, Theodoros Stamos, Paul Goodman, Anaïs Nin, Albert Einstein and
                   Jack Tworkov, among others.

                   Rauschenberg, Dorothea Rockburne, Michael Rumaker, Cy Twombly, Kenneth Snelson, Susan Weil and Vera Williams.

                  expansion) that affected the future of the college to the length of its history.



                   The BMC considered itself hereditary to the Bauhaus2, so much for the idea of an
                  experimental art school, as a direct way through some of its integrants that formed part of
                  other institutions, such as Josef and Anni Albers, Xanti Schawinsky, Walter Gropius,
                  Marcel Breuer, Lyonel Feininger and Stefan Wolpe , there are several compromise levels

                   methods employed by others of them, han sido tratados con mayor or less profundidad, la arquitectura, como discipline, y
                   los arquitectos que allí trabajaron han sido esbozados solo en la periferia de las aproximaciones artísticas made in the
                   center.




                   This job originates from personal interests in the middle of the institution. However, mainly,
                   from the opportunity to deepen the architecture as the area of knowledge in the BMC that,
                   without having a regular presence, consiguió encontrar su lugar en el college, así como
                   de explorar los logros conseguidos a través de su doctrine y vinculación with the rest of
                   the supplied materials. It also arises from the conviction of understanding architecture as
                   an activity that took place at the time , at the moment and at the same time, in direct
                   relation to experimentation and reality, that this institution is a singular example. The
                   architecture is conceived from the interdisciplinariedad, abarcando distinct modes of
                   expression in a continuous process of learning.

                   Interdisciplinariedad as part of a vital desarrollo donde, as in my case, dance is part of my

                   OBJECTIVES


                   As it was stated in principle, this is what you intend to study and learn
                   Learn about architecture in the BMC. A discipline that you study for your program, your actions and your
                   results. Furthermore, we analyzed the presence of architects in the institution, to determine the design
                   wallpaper and evaluate its applications, as well as the architectural, pre-existing and constructed property,
                   of the campus, as the educational scenario. For this reason it is necessary to approach, first of all, the
                   methodology and educational organization of the institution of the propio temporal framework in order to
                   emerge and reveal the unique characteristics of the project. In the period of time between 1911, he saw
                   Walter Gropius and Adolf Meyer build the Fagus factory, before he saw the BMC disappear, 1957, he
                   trained him to design architecture and experiment with multiple changes. A time of convulsion of World
                   War II, from 1914 to 1945, which, without embargo, does not impide the desarrollo of architectural
                   representatives adecuadas to a new society.




                  Also dealing with the design of the architecture as a design, most of it is
                   limit diffusion with other disciplines, aspect that, in the case of the BMC studio, manifested itself in a very
                   relevant way. The investigation claims to demonstrate the architectural discipline as a series of successes
                   and experiments that make possible significant experiences. For a while, there is an exploration,
                   compilation and revision of the achievements, from the point of view of the field of knowledge of
                   architecture in relation to the rest of the arts and activities that are discovered in the BMC. Therefore, we
                   proceed to detect and establish the characteristics and the identifying patterns that are manifested in the
                   distinct selected actions.

                  There are parallels between the educational methods of the Bauhaus and the BMC,
                  mainly in terms of the interdisciplinary integration of artistic activity with real experience.
                  To demonstrate this idea, it has been explored in its origins and connections, recognizing
                  the repercusión that occurs through the disintegration of the institution and its projection
                  through the influence occasioned by the diaspora of professors and students, followers
                  of its procedures and lectures.
                  Deepening this type of transoceanic connections, you can also identify the vinculos
                  establecidos con España through the figures involucradas in the college. Finally, it is
                  claimed to evaluate the educational method all implemented, destacando sus éxitos,
                  but also desvelando sus riesgos y fracasos.

                  Our main interests are in your current life, medio siglo después, how to reflect on plants
                  and how to confront education and profession. Ideas that inseparably vindicate a
                  concept of architecture “abierta al suceso” al resto de disciplines desde de una mirada
                  transversal. This is work that discovers the importance and future implications of
                  architectural activity in the BMC, understanding how to catalyze an opportunity space
                  and an inclusive activity within the educational program, related to the exclusive training
                  that is offered in the actuality of the majority of programs and schools.




                  This text also claims to be an acercamiento, a recovery of the sucedido in the BMC, a
                  new language, because most of the information relating to this institution is found edited
                  in English, but it does not contain a second translation of the published version. ,
                  otherwise an investigation in situ -in the sede of its archivo and of the college- that
                  completes, clarifies and reveals the reality, and in order, large part of the mito, to the
                  vez that reveals its architectural component.




                   DOCUMENT STRUCTURE


                   This present text is organized in very different blocks and structured in four chapters.
                   The first block refers to one of the introductory notes that constitute the first chapter (1º);
                   The second block is the central part of the discourse, which is revealed in three more
                   chapters (2º, 3º and 4º); There is a block of conclusions. This is the summary of the
                   presentation and, finally, the bibliography and the sources, as well as the annexes of
                   files and the diagrams prepared by the author for a more easy understanding of the
                   entire cantidad of data, protagonists and successes.



                  The introductory notes (which are presented as the first chapter) adopt the title:
                  Education on a mountain, in reference to the article published in 1936 by Louis Adamic4
                  and which was proporcionó publicidad and difusión al proyecto. This first chapter must
                  be understood as a first approximation to the BMC in which it is
                  can differ in the parts. Firstly, we will address the temporal context of the birth of the
                  institution and the participants who formed the project; This is a college studio in the
                  North American universities and experimental schools, as well as the stories of the

                   METHODOLOGY


                  Se ha proceeded to carry out a historical investigation of bibliography and hemeroteca on
                  the BMC to, subsequently, compare and revise the information in the archives and
                  documentary sources, all the primary sources consulted in situ.
                  First of all, we created a bibliographic collection of monographic publications from the BMC,
                  including catalogs of artistic, collective and individual exhibitions, many of our participants as
                  well as articles from which the institution is present. The difficulty of accessing the original
                  sources, both due to the geographical distance and the use of the college installations ,
                  requires one of the expanded plazos to lift the cab to this end.

                   Access to the information begins within a field job that involves access to the original source, through

                                                                                                     , The Western Regional
                  Archives (in conjunction with the WRA) contains the original archival material which contains much of the
                  available material and which serves as the basis for this investigation.
                  Then there was a view of the campus where the college was located . The campuses, the Blue Ridge
                   Assembly and the installations of Lago Eden are in perfect condition and are in operation, without any
                   modifications. This camp work is completed with visits to the Black Mountain College Museum and Arts
                   Center, a small museum dedicated to the BMC, the Swannanoa Valley Museum, which contains and
                   exhibits documentation of the local history of the Buncombe building and the Asheville Art Museum,
                   which alberga obra gráfica, pictórica y photos de algunos participantes en el college.




                   In Europe we took a trip to Berlin to visit the Black Mountain exhibition. An interdisciplinary Experiment

                   -Museum für Gegenwart from June 5 to September 27, 2015, you can contact the team of professor Dr.

                   participants more fully understood and also includes works by Fuller, Schawinsky and plans from the
                   projects of Walter Gropius and Marcel Breuer. As part of the exhibition, we organized the Black Mountain–
                   Educational Turn and the Avantgarde8 exhibition , which was attended and attended by the exhibition.




                   Over the course of the days, European and North American participants approached several approaches
                   to the investigation into the art.


                   Another fundamental source for this work is the data obtained from specialized publications and
                   audiovisual documents from the various archives and libraries available, as well as the revision of specific
                  previous investigations on the material or which is vinculadas in the tangential theme.


                   Among the libraries visited are the Ramsey Library of the University of North Carolina, which has

                  BACKGROUND AND ESTADO OF CUESTIÓN


                   The main part of the existing investigations is that the BMC is centered on the plastic arts, especially on
                   the abstract expression of North America. This is where the BMC has a clear representation of many of
                   its most relevant figures such as Willem de Kooning, Franz Kline or Robert Motherwell, who, without
                  llegar a profundizar en la enseñanza artística en el college, siempre destacaron su paso por el mismo
                   as a fundamental part in addition to trayectorias. In the various exhibitions and catalogs associated with
                   various artists such as previously cited and other many such as Josef Albers, Ray Johnson, Ruth Asawa
                   or Kenneth Snelson, to mention several more, the college appears as such of the background, but
                   without carrying information on what follows. allí.




                  Algo similarly follows with music, perhaps in this center we find a variety of highly innovative figures like
                  John Cage or David Tudor, but at the same time, it was a source of European music that was forced to
                   emigrate like Heinrich Jalowetz, Erwin Bodky oStefan Wolpe.




                   In the ambito of literature, the contribution of the college is very significant, especially in the ambito of
                   poetry lasting the last years in which the college is operational. This camp was recognized and studied
                  by the group that arose at the institution called Black Mountain.

                   1.1 IDEAL CONTEXT AND TEMPORAL SPACE



                  The birth of the BMC in 1933 was linked to the developments that succeeded both in
                  Europe and in the USA. that conditioned on its origin, desarrollo and disappearance.
                  The economic crisis and the high number of examples in the USA, derived from the
                  crack of 1929, led to the cultivation of the new educational tendencies that were
                  promulgated as a major freedom. Some difficult moments, with emerging ideas for
                  educational reforms and teaching methods. Coinciding with the appearance of the
                  BMC, Franklin D. Roosevelt, new elected president of the EU, launched the PWAP,
                  Public Works Art Project which soon transformed into the WPA/FAP, Work Progress
                  Administration's Federal Arts Project, an institution for to share wines between culture
                  and cities, through the creation of galleries and art museums, theater groups and
                                                                                            1
                  orchestras among others                                                     .


                  In Europe, the Bauhaus consolidated itself as a reference of the artistic teachings of
                  the vanguards congregating with professional relationships with the arts, design and
                  architecture, coming from Central Europe and Rusia, along with its different stages,
                  localizations and leaders. Following Hitler's legacy in Germany in 1933 and the
                  Bauhaus movement, some of their members were forced to march to Europe, exile
                  and reside in the USA; in Chicago, Boston, Massachusetts, New York and Carolina
                  del Norte, principally, influenced by the North American culture2 .
                                                                                    In the case of the
                  BMC, at the age of 1941, I joined some European refugees (profesores and families)
                  by academic and humanitarian circles3 and those of the 1938-39 academic course,
                  and the course of 1942 also joined three refugee students.

                  In the case of Spain, with the Civil War (1935-39), the exile of some clave figures was
                  produced in the EE.UU. participating in some of the items in the BMC project and
                  which are more specific in this work.

                  In these moments and parallel forms, we unravel the activities and interchanges of
                  ideas and propose them through cultural organizations and universities. In Chicago,
                  with the Illinois Institute of Technology, the New Bauhaus and the Institute of Design.
                  In Boston, he worked at the Universities of Harvard and the Massachusetts Institute of
                  Technology. In New York as a clave city, through the University of Yale and especially
                  through art galleries like Art of This Century (registered by Peggy Guggenheim),
                  Charles Egan, Sam Kootz, Betty Parsons and the Museo de Arte Moderno, MoMA.

                   1.2 HISTORIES THAT CONFORM TO THE LEYENDA




                   The educational project that we occupy is located in Black Mountain, a village located near Asheville,
                  in the condado of Buncombe, in the state of Carolina del Norte, characterized by its natural landscape
                  and its thermal waters, is a vacation destination for those who buscan descanso y aire puro. The center
                  is located in a natural enclave, close to the National Park Blue Ridge Parkway, about 26 km from the
                  center of the city of Asheville.




                   In these moments, Carolina del Norte was a very conservative state, with the fact that, from the
                  principio, the BMC was considered a liberal center attentive to its methodology, but especially by its
                   community character, which was associated with determined political ideas, acusando a our members
                  of the communists. Aside from being a mixed community, sleeping areas are separated by sex, even
                  though we don't know the time, we see rumors, some confirmations, parties, meetings, homosexual
                  relationships and relationships between the professor and the BMC15 alumna .




                  Its remote location, located in the natural environment, has a certain distance from nearby populations,
                   as well as the notable presence of teachers and professional teachers from various places, generating
                   a kind of story in the form of local rumors magnified from numerous anecdotes. of the more variopinto.
                   Rumors that llegaban the village of Black Mountain, he llegaba affirmed that the BMC was a community
                  of free love and nudist16. Detentions of professors like Robert Wunsch, rector and professor of
                   literature, in 1945, for “crime against nature”, when he was encountered in the Asheville traffic in his
                   vehicle with a marine17; o of the alumni, Barbara Anderson and Jeanne Wacker, traveled on the road
                   and were accused of prostitution18. Love stories between teachers and students like José de Creeft19
                  with his alumna, Lore Goulet, with whom he was at home, and the infidelities of teachers like Dreier20
                  and Olson21 with other women, other students; o activities largely abiertas sobre su sexuality, comme
                  la de Paul Goodman, profesor de literatur y escritura en el verano de 1950, fueron controvertidas y
                  participan en la creation de layenda del BMC. Histories with eco-friendly materials appearing in books
                  with a large autobiographical component

                   1.3 HISTORY: THE NORTH AMERICA UNIVERSITY AND LAS ESCUELAS
                   EXPERIMENTAL




                   The university, as an educational center, must provide a significant learning value for life, both as part
                  of the city and society as a professional level. Therefore, I start from the idea that in the university we
                  do not teach, otherwise we learn, we assume. The university must support student self-training with
                   suggestions, recommendations, training routes, etc. From active learning the student goes on to
                  mature and accumulate academic experiences. A continuation was made of a revision of the American
                   University and of the experimental schools, between which we encountered the BMC.




                   The historical origin of the North American universities goes back to s. XVII, inspired by various
                   aspects of English, French and French universities, also, with other influences from other European
                  universities. Desde sus inicios y hasta hace relativamente poco tiempo (finals of s.


                  XX), centering on organizational efforts and expansion mainly in the elites. From the age of 70, we
                  began to expand our public spectrum potential, open our arms to higher general education and
                   promote universal access to schools and universities. Please note that all education in the USA,
                   including higher education, depends on local and federal governments, without the existence of a
                  central ministry of education encargado de una gestión global y strategica de la misma.




                   The first university center in the USA was Harvard College, founded in 1636 by the number of New
                   College, Massachusetts, and which changed its number to the present in 1939. From entonces, to
                   finals del s. XVIII and principles of the s. XIX, we found various universities in the most recent states:
                  Universities of Georgia, Carolina del Norte, Carolina del Sur, Alabama, Tennessee and Virginia.



                  Through the Becas Morris Land Law of 1862, higher public education benefited from the acquisition of
                   ingredients through the sale of lands not colonized in the West. This unique condition applies to some
                  of the ingredients and is used in higher education centers. As a result of this, we create a number of
                  universities and we equip ourselves with new statuses that exist, differentiating public universities from
                   private ones.



                   In 1900 the AAU, American University Association, was created , an institution that contributed
                  intensely to raising academic levels and bringing them closer to the standard of European universities.
                  The period between 1870 and 1910 is considered the era of the construction of the North American
                  universities. In fact, we are able to attract highly talented graduates and professors who have raised
                   large campuses with donations and industrial industries from sectors such as petroleum, metallurgy,
                  construction and transportation. This type of private financing, part of large fundaciones filantrópicas
                  sans fines lucrative, fue esencial a lo largo de la primera mitad del s.XX, sobre todo en las
                  universidades que necesitaban amplar su investigación enspecializada. Among them are the
                  Rockefeller Foundation, the General Council of Rockefeller Education, the Carnegie Corporation and
                   the Ford Foundation.




                                                                       49

--- Page 51 ---
Machine Translated by Google




                   During the World War II, our American and Canadian governments worked coordinated with a good
                  number of universities and our educational teams to initiate investigative projects related to various
                  forms with the content. This is the case for laboratories and teams trained in chemicals and physics
                  that work in the implementation of new arms like the hydrogen bomb. The projects carried out by
                  scientists and specialists at universities have unpredictable results and will continue in a permanent
                   manner, supporting the creation of federal agencies such as the NSF, National Science Foundation
                   and the NIH, National Institute of Health, which will provide the necessary investigative efforts to
                   this end. universities.




                  It's the place where many North American universities, among them and the BMC, offer to support
                  specialists exiled from Europe, inviting them to our faculties of the EU and providing them with an
                  inyección of talent that contributes to transforming the profile of the students they have conformaban.



                   From its origins, the BMC follows the traditional conception of the majority of North American
                   universities, opting for an educational plan in the liberal arts30, liberal arts education, of experimental
                  nature, of an interdisciplinar character and with an informal spirit. A college whose knowledge is
                  acquired through experience, observation and the development of critical thinking, including the
                  words of Rice31 and the formation of part of the community that contributes to log educational
                  objects to form a social unity that involucraba all of them We are part of a participatory and
                  interactive manner. For Rice the practice of the art, especially for those with a rare natural talent
                  for the mismo, will be the best way to interiorize the importance of the method and the process to
                   achieve the results you can obtain.

                  Superada the influence of the School of Fine Arts in the architectural teaching of the American

                   de Chicago, which crystaliza in figures like Richardson or Adler and Sullivan . The first architectural
                  school in the USA, the Massachusetts Institute of Technology (MIT), was founded in Boston in 1865.



                   For another reason, there exists at this moment in the country a series of alternative educational
                   theories and institutions that decisively influence the germination of the creation of new educational
                   projects between those that are taught by the BMC.


                  It was the first direct influence of the appearance of Brook Farm32, founded in 1841 by George and
                  Sophia Ripley in Massachusetts. It is a private community, based on the principles of Charles
                  Fourier, with schools where there is an active participation, through convivencia and physical work
                  in the field, as the BMC repeats itself. Cerró sus puertas in 1847.

                   Furthermore, the birth of The Child-Centered School was later named The Self-Centered School33, an

                   The figure of Mark Hopkins40, teólogo and pedagogo, can be considered as another influence on the

                  Another significant example we found in The Rollins College, founded in 1885 in Florida. A school of arts

                  1.4 DEWEY EXPERIENCE THEORY AND EXPERIENCE SPACE
                  LEARNING



                  This apart, we approach the theoretical learning models, and link them to the lifts in
                  the BMC. The educational methodology all applied, which is discussed in the next
                  chapter, has a very experimental character, which each individual follows on its own
                  itinerary according to their interests, abilities and compromises. A combination of
                  teachings from paths, conferences, classes and discussions comes from mismas, in
                  other architectural and natural scenarios, which foment creativity and the sight of
                  miracles.



                  From the new science of learning, new science of learning, one of the lessons is
                  experiential learning. The Theory of Experiential Learning, alongside TAE, was learned
                  by John Dewey in 193856 and has a strong relationship with the basic principles and
                  methodology employed in the BMC. The theories of Dewey are fundamental to the
                  time of conforming the educational and educational project of the college.



                  In the TAE, Dewey reveals the potential of experience through interaction with the
                  medium and distinguishes four phases in his model of learning: concrete experience,
                  reflection, abstract conceptualization and application. According to this model, learning
                  begins with a concrete experience that, through reflection and conceptualization, is
                  interpreted by the individual. In the application, the ability to transfer awareness to
                  other situations is demonstrated. It is a cycle process where four phases are interrelated
                  and, as you can see in the diagram, the learning process requires the integration of
                  each of them. To decide, so that knowledge can be acquired, the intervention and
                  involvement of the individual is necessary, and it is necessary to feel the experience
                  through the relationship that establishes itself with its anticipated knowledge and
                  desarrollando conceptual structures that allow it to be applied to various situations. So,
                  no more than one experience to provoke awareness57.



                  This theory reveals a holistic learning model that is generated by the transformation of
                  experience and that it was investigated, not alone by Dewey, but also by Kurt Lewin,
                  Jena Piaget, William James, Carl Jung, Paulo Freire and Carl Rogers among others .



                  La TAE, part of the article by Alice and David Kolb58, is based on the figures previously
                  mentioned, and is built on six propositions: 1. The learning must
                      be conceived as a process and not in the bush and obtaining results. It is “the
                          process through which knowledge is created through the transference of
                          experience”59.

                       2. Everything you learn is re-learned. In other words: it deals with a process of
                           deepening and integration into new knowledge to discover new ideas.

                       3. Learning requires the resolution of dialectical conflicts respecting the different
                           modes of adaptation to the world. In learning, one must be placed in shirts
                           where there is action and reflection, and intuition and thought.

                       4. Learning is a holistic process of adaptation to the world that involves all the
                           integrated functions of the individual: thought, feeling, perception and
                           behavior.
                       5. Learning is the result of the synergies that are established between the person
                           and their environment. A word from Piaget: “Learning occurs through balance
                           in a dialectical process of assimilating new experiences together with existing
                           concepts and adapting existing concepts to new experiences”

                       6. Learn is the process of creating knowledge, which is contraponed to the start
                           of learning by transmission of the ideas prefijadas son transmitted to the
                           student. Experimental learning proposes a constructivist foundation of the
                           mismo in which the knowledge is created and recreated in the knowledge of
                           the learner.

                   The awareness, entonces, is created through the perception of the experience and
                   the process of the misma. All TAE proposals are implicit in ideological thinking and
                   between the objects of the BMC.

                  The TAE, according to Kolb's model, explains that there are dialéctic models of
                  percibir la experiencia (Experiencia Concreta y Conceptualización Abstracta) and
                  other dos of procesar dicha experiencia (Observación Reflexiva y Experimentación
                  Activa). These four functions or models correspond to four regions of the cerebral
                  cortex: frontal cortex (abstracción), premotor and motor cortex (active experience),
                  sensory area (perception), temporal lobe (capacity of reflection).



                  The TAE is defined through development in three stages that are characterized by
                  the complexity and relativism of the time of adaptation to the world and the integration
                  of conflicts between dialectical models to perceive and transform experience. These
                  are the following steps: (1) acquisition that includes being born before adolescence,
                  absorbing basic skills and learning cognitive structures; (2) specialization, which
                  includes from the stage of education before adult education when experimenting with
                  social and educational organization and (3) integration from adult education to adult
                  education, when non-dominant learning models express themselves in social
                  relationships y en el trabajo.

                   The investigations in the field of learning show how it exists in four styles of mismo. There are several
                  ways to perceive and process information that depends on the characteristics of each individual
                  depending on genetic heritage, life experiences and environment. From all previous information,
                  preferences are revealed in our new perception and the transformation of the information. Each
                  individual person is the result of personal conflict and conflict at the moment, whether it is abstract or
                   concrete or reflexive activities. Estos estilos son divergent, asimilador, convergente y acomodable.
                   Every individual manifests the components of four styles and variations that will affect the learning
                  process.




                   An individual with a divergent style posits Concrete Experience and Reflexive Observation as dominant
                   skills. Seen in situations from various points of view and pose great skill in situations that require
                  generating ideas, brainstorming. She is a very imaginative person with diverse cultural interests,
                  tending to specialize in the field of art and working with a team to observe distinct points of view and
                  feed back the distinct comments.




                   In the individual assimilator it appears as the dominant style of Abstract Conceptualization and Reflexive
                  Observation. Destaca en la comprensión de la información que procesan de manera concise y lógica,
                  mostrando interés en ideas y conceptos abstractos. There is a tendency to develop and train in
                  technical skills.


                  Aquellos con convergent style poses in the Abstract Conceptualization and the Experimentation
                   activates as the dominant learning styles. You have special skills for problem solving and many
                   decisions for the solution collection, and are designed to help you learn theoretical and abstract
                   concepts. You tend to have technical careers and expertise, opting for experimentation with new ideas
                  and laboratory practices.




                   Finally, the individual with this accomodating style has the dominant skills to learn Concrete Experience
                  and Active Experimentation.
                  Learn practical experience, learn to learn practical planes and face new facts. You tend to actuate
                  movies by instinct more than from an analytical approximation, so that in your working environment you
                  have to follow practical application paths that include field work and a batch of different methods to
                  complete a project.




                  Según Kolb, recent studies demonstrate that these four learning styles can be amplified as soon as
                  possible. David Hunt and his team60 (Abby, Hunt and Weiser, 1985) identified other four and, in 2002,
                  Mainemelis61, Boyatziz and Kolb also broke the organization into four predefined and stereotypical
                   categories. The styles can be viewed in Figure 1.18.

                   Learning Spaces Following the

                  model proposed by Kolb, introduce the concept of learning space to approach the complete and dynamic nature of individuals,

                  processes and the twists and turns that it unfolds. Spaces that do not belong to our physics, but which build the experience

                   in the environment including the processes of socialization, but all of the ambito of the family, compare the principles with the

                  methodology of the BMC that most of it is approached.




                  There are four different learning areas:

                         Vital Space (EV)
                         The vital space, life space, is a concept developed from the work of Kurt Lewin,
                         where the persona and its interdependent variables are involved.
                         The behavior translates to the mathematics and manifests itself as the function
                         of the variables listed: B=f(p,e).
                         Lewin affirmed that the vital space of the individual is composed of the ambitos
                         that conform to your perception of the world, tal like deseos, necesidades,
                         creencias, influences, recuerdos y acontecimientos de naturaleza politica,
                         economica y social. This space is determined by the contraposition of internal
                         needs and external demands. The vital space can be understood topologically
                         as a map which distinguishes the dimension and relative positions, balance of
                         positions, areas of relationship and conflict, etc.



                         Bronfenbrenner Anidada Structure (EA)
                         Urie Bronfrenbrenner's68 realizes a study on the ecology of the learning spaces,
                         representing as a structure anidada jerarquizada composed of: Microsistema
                         que se dearrolla
                             en un torno o próximo scenario (como el aula o el curso)

                               My system was formed by other competing scenarios in my life and in the
                               context of other courses, familiar relationships, etc.
                               Exosistema that builds the social structure that directly influences the
                               personality's environment as it affects administrative policies and campus
                               culture.
                               Macrosystem that refers to the patronages and institutional values at a
                               general cultural level, as it prioritizes theoretical learning in practice.

                   1.5 BMC EDUCATIONAL METHODS



                                              “The work of individual children is double, you must learn your skills

                  To understand the BMC and all that follows, we need to take into account the
                  teaching methods that are based on educational activity. Según Rice78, the object
                  will be taught the method and no content, haciendo hincapié en el proceso para que
                  los alumnos no fuesen meros receptores pasivos de información, sino productivos
                  y creativos. Note the essential difference where the BMC relates to the idea of
                  obtaining or receiving training, realizing an opportunity to experiment with the
                  educational process. This process, a mode of mental training, has great importance
                  to the imagination as the motor of consciousness. The imagination as the same that
                  must be undertaken to develop the full, creative and productive nature of the
                  persona. One of the planted objects was to create a synergy that continues between
                  the experience of the day and the art, transforming and expanding the established
                  limits between disciplines and activities. Buscar a balance between emotions and
                  intellect.79

                  For her, Rice apostaba for personal freedom to self-organize and will be applied
                  both to students and to teachers80. A self-learning process that unfolds through
                  interpersonal relationships, avoiding Islam and enmity. In the BMC we tried to avoid
                  judging students in the context of the aula, to appreciate the qualities and personal
                  talents that, generally, we do not recognize them in the traditional academic criteria.
                  This is how Xanti Schawinsky explains his memory of his educational experience in
                  the center, related to the training of the rest of the universities that took him to his
                  students, and the BMC took the green 81.




                                              “You are the curriculum you make…”82

                  Any activity or job that you want to do in college is understood as part of the
                  educational process, but not as training for a professional career. The curriculum
                  will be flexible83, with the possibility of adapting to possible unforeseen circumstances
                  and allowing the student to organize herself according to her proposed work plan
                  according to her interests.

                                       “Educate the student as a person and as a city84”.


                   The BMC advertised itself in its final stage, in 1949, as a progressive college , whose idealism
                  functioned and as a place that offered a balance between the artistic curriculum, the academic and
                  the practical knowledge. In the letter it is distinguished as the same characteristics of the BMC, and
                  that it is a educational center, for women and men, no sectarian, with a high academic level, without
                  racial or religious beliefs, without fraternities or inter-university deportes.


                   For another reason, we removed our work program, the large extension of the campus property,
                  approximately 600 acres (243 Ha.) and the individualized studio program85.



                   The BMC is considered a continuation of the Bauhaus, in the sense that it was built in great measure
                  with its experiences and with the European traditions of the Bauhaus, through the distinct figures that
                  were brought to the college of all, especially that of Josef Albers86 . Por otro lado, exist en abass
                  instituciones el esfuerzo de educar desde de unfoque holístico. Although Bauhaus is centered on
                  design, art, architecture and BMC as a global education of the individual, it does not depend on the
                  causal-effect relationship with the production of artists, designers or professionals related to it. ambito
                  artistico87. At the same time, we were encouraged by the students to think and act on them, even
                  though they differed from the Bauhaus, and the BMC did not see itself as a school of art.




                   The access criteria to enter a form part of the BMC are varied. You can access your course directly
                  from any secondary school course or proceed to your university center. If you do not certify in the
                  past, you will carry out an examination of the central information in the language and mathematics
                  signatures. From 1940, this examination was obligatory for every student who joined the college. With
                  this medida, it claims to differ between three types of students: (1) those who have studied secondary
                   school, (2) those who do not graduate in secondary school and (3), por ultimo, those students who do
                  not have completed the official college examination88 . So that you do not approve the exam and
                  therefore do not want to enter the college, the center prepares an advanced course with the aim of
                   learning the required knowledge before starting the academic course.

                   aficionados and extracurricular activities, as well as references from professors, medical examinations

                  The community, understanding life, work and study at BMC. The institution was formed as a place for

                   a little scale, with a location in a remote location108 and where we will meet more than 100 students at

                   A form of life within a social unit109 is truly tejida, with the defects in its voluntary sector. An organization
                  founded on individual freedom and responsibility in an environment of collaboration between all its
                   components. In an environment of creative freedom, education and election also for alumni. Una
                  libertad de la that cada uno a nivel individual es responsible y aprende de ella.




                                        “Liberty…no es easy, ya se sabe. The result is that it is difficult to learn how to use
                                        it as it is controlled” 110.


                   Rice asked for his ideal of community that resulted in a mix between flexibility and order, where all
                  decisions were made by consent and his study and life were intertwined. Living in community
                  contributes to the lograr of educational objects, transforming it into a social unity that involves all of our
                  extensive relationships through participation and interaction112. Se derriba la distinción entre alumno
                  y profesor, para sustituirse por la idea de relación entre membros de una misma comunidad, sin
                   diferenciar el trabajo del aula de lo que ocurre fuera de ella. The difference between curricular and
                   extracurricular activities is revealed, as if the “work” within and will be affected by their aulas. All the
                  members of the community work together. Across the academic curriculum, there is a week, called
                  Interlude System113, in which the collective classes are interrupted to dedicate this time to exclusive
                  work and individual studies.

                  of some anarquía119.

                  spatial organization of the architecture of the campus, which propiciaba el encuentro e intercambio en
                  un ambiente “casi pre-hippie beatnik”120.




                  <DEMOCRACY>
                  The BMC was an institution founded on democracy. A democratic organizational structure, not
                  jerarquizada and self-governed, all of which is elected by consent from the assemblies that exist as a
                   directive council, called Board of Fellows. This directive was formed by a rector (avoid the title of
                  president, which was extended to educational councils), where professors and the presence of an
                   alumnus were elected by our fellow students, in the delegated mode, with you in the positions that We
                  are interested as students in the training process.

                   The meetings of the Board of Fellows are weekly and if they are held as relevant, the meeting extends
                   to the entire BMC community. A self-governed structure, with the council of professors already involved
                  in the organizational structure of the college, so many educational questions as well as those relating
                  to its property.


                   The cargoes of representation are temporal, with a duration of three years.
                  This is the period when elections are called, so please form part of the professor's colleagues that you
                   desire. In this way, the representative cargoes (rector, secretary, supervisor) and administrators are
                   distributed among all the professors of the community. The college, through the Board of Fellows, has
                  control over the educational program, personnel contract, financial control and administration of the
                  center122.




                   In the early stages of the BMC, from 1933 to 1943, there existed a group of professionals, who formed
                  part of the Board of Fellows, decisive at the time of their formation. These include John A. Rice,
                  Theodore Dreier, Frederick Georgia, Frederick Mangold, Robert Wunsch and Erwin Strauss, who were
                  later relegated to seize their cargo ships. For another part, Josef Albers and Theodore Dreier joined
                   the Board of Fellows for five years, actively participating in the management and governance of the
                  college.


                   The first rector of the BMC was Frederick Georgia, who was during his first year of existence, when he
                  left the academic plan. Subsequently, Rice was elected to the cargo ship in 1938 for four years.


                   Subsequently, Robert Wunsch was elected for three years, until 1945. The figure of the BMC tester
                  was originally thought to have been rotated for three years, but Theodore Dreier was for three years,
                  from 1933 to 1945.
                  When Wunsch abandoned the BMC and his rector's cargo, Dreier left it for years. The BMC secretary
                  has one of his duties to write the acts of the Board of Fellows and receive the official correspondence
                  of the college.
                   Fueron secretarios Frederick Georgia (1933–1937) Frederick Mangold (1937–
                   1942) John Evarts and Lawrence Kocher continued during the course of 1942-43.


                   Externally, there existed a council of consultants, Advisory Council, which replaced the administrative
                  figure of Trustees of other university centers. In the case of the BMC, it was formed by representatives
                  of the educational field, artists and scientists of number, who supervise the educational and monitoring
                  positions, carry out a follow-up and in direct contact with all that they succeed, without legal authority.
                  This committee included John Dewey, Walter Gropius, Carl Jung, Franz Kline, Max Lerner, John
                  Burchard and Albert Einstein, among others. Between 1933 and 1943 it was formed by veinticinco my
                  embros with different profiles, which was formally attached to the college to know the first man of its
                   activities and functions, in addition to being informed by the periodic management of the one that all
                  succeeded. This advice, in some cases, includes the BMC prestigio and the consejeros actuaban like
                   our embajadores for the different states.

                  The courses offered are organized into a structure that covers the stages, junior
                  division and senior division.

                  The junior division constitutes a first period in the college studies, with a duration of
                  approximately two years, whereby through the distinct areas of knowledge it is decided
                  by some one to start in the senior course. The areas of knowledge are divided into
                  attributes of natural sciences, social sciences, humanities and literature and art.



                  This superior course is accessed through a written and oral exam, senior division
                  exam, developed by the professor of all the areas involved and with external evaluators,
                  which evaluates the abilities and knowledge of the alumni. The object was to
                  compromise the student's maturity, showing his ability to reflect and relate to ideas in
                  a transversal manner, because the proposed statements do not necessarily have to be
                  shared throughout the course of the course127.

                  a lo largo de dos jornadas, con acceso a la biblioteca y sus recursos. You can choose

                  senior course. The duration of the exam will be one, two or three days, one or two

                  recommended for the candidate.134

                  <INTERDISCIPLINARY>
                  The concept of interdisciplinarity, based on the constant collaboration between plants and activities of
                  the art at the center of education, resulted in one of the pillars of the educational methodology of the
                   BMC. In college, art centered on the work of plastic arts, but also on music and theater. Debería
                  includes in this group the approximation that is carried out by the work with materials such as the
                  textile design and the architecture.



                   The collaborations are established between the different artistic and scientific disciplines in a
                  complementary way, avoiding Islam and diffusing the limits, which enriches the process and the final
                   result. The art as the main signature of the BMC curriculum, is experimented by all students, aspiring
                   artists or non-artists, and is understood from the creative experience, which enhances all academic
                  interests or knowledge areas. Professors have the role of guides for students who participate in
                  multiple courses and interdisciplinary seminars.




                                       “A need to discover new directions through the reading of all the arteries that exist
                                       parallel to the time of confronting structures, limits, syntax, beginnings and
                                       endings”140.


                   The studio and practice of distinct artistic manifestations are considered an essential part of the BMC
                   student's educational curriculum. It is important to consider that none of the founders of the college are
                  artists or professors of art and that the object of the mismo is not based on the creation of artists, if not
                  the consideration of all individuals as potential creators. Apostaban por el arte como herramienta para
                  despertar el intellecto, como vía de resolución de problems y como fuente de cognimiento.




                   La interdisciplinariedad produces a plurality of sources of information, which makes it possible to learn
                   and discover through a multiplicity of experiences, integrating artistic activity with real experience. An
                  interdisciplinary that extends to the forms of verbal, artistic, theatrical, and performative expressions
                   and that embraces a continuous interchange of experiences, exploring the established limits to modify
                  them. In these interdisciplinary schools we developed innovative spatial-temporal projects and models,
                  in the pursuit of new creative dynamics in art, science and education.




                   The BMC artist embraces science and humanity, avoiding conflicts of interest in the program, so that
                   students have the opportunity to participate in music, theater, dance and art classes141. It includes
                  classes of economics, psychology, philosophy, history, science, biology, chemistry, physics,
                  mathematics and languages (Latin, Greek, Spanish, French and German among others). Philosophy
                   and social sciences permit the study of the eternal questions of humanity, but of the student's
                  approximation to their responsibilities, decisions propias and uncertainties. The art is

                  <ACCIÓN>
                   Finally, we encounter the action as it will be positioned, resulting from observation and experimentation. The
                  experiment is a recurrent idea in the discussions relating to this institution as much in the educational environment,
                   the life in community as in the art. Mixed tradition and innovation and conversion to the avant-garde of art and
                  progressive political ideas146.

             1.6 THE BMC CAMPUS




             The campus, as a recent university student, takes into account the various activities involved in teaching, and has in
             the BMC a special importance for the relevance that it offers, in its definition, in the community aspect as one of the
             bases of its teaching method. It is possible to understand the campus as the global scenario in which the learning
             experience is built and that it is not limited to the physical space but includes the aspects of socialization and the
             relationship between the members of the community and the institutions.




             The physical space in education, in this case of university, is approached to highlight the internal scenario of the
             architectural pieces of the campus, as well as the external, its insertion in an urbanistic style. From this approximation
             to the physical structure, we observe the evolution that has experimented with the university of its origin in the
             monasteries of Edad Media, where the claustro will era the heart where the common life will be concentrated.
             Subsequently, in the stages of Renacimiento and of Barroco, there is a change in the understanding of knowledge,
             abandoning the absolutism atribuido al saber teológico and adapting the format edificado, associating with the new
             ideologies and plants. From now on, new types of organization based on the aggregation of independent architectural
             pieces, the evolution of our ideal ones, structures and spatial configurations. La convivencia y dualidad en la relation
             de estas edificaciones con el tejido urbano se nos muestra a través de recintos universitarios en los cascos históricos
             frente a grande organizaciones de nueva planta en las periferias, que son el reflejo de la propia evolución de las
             universidades.




             The North American university model has experimented with a changing literature from its origins and has shown a
             strong influence in various international academic studies. The terminus of campus 161 appears firstly in a letter in
             Princeton, with a letter written in 1774, in which an alumnus used it to describe a fire on the extension of the central
             building, Nassau Hall. Other interpretations of the term relate to the idea of a large space loaded with cultural
             significance, related to the voice Campus Martius, Campo de Marte.




             The origin of the North American campus is part of the model of the British college , both in addition to its formal
                                                                 162
             aspects as English functionalities, which                 . Desde los primeros asentamientos en Nueva
             apostaban for higher education, how to proliferate the colleges
             colonial during the 17th century. Eran unas organizations disseminated by the territory with a self-sufficient character,
             implanted in the margins of the city and which were disembodied in the academic populations, academic village, as for
             example the University of Virginia (1817).

             In the case of BMC, our university installations are established, like all of the North American campuses, in natural
             environments where the presence of nature is very significant, generating a species of academic microcosmos. In
             this sense, it is interesting to approach the relationship that is established between the educational model that is
             proposed with the physical context and the architectural configuration that I envision.



             In a first step, the BMC established its campus, and was founded in 1933, in the Blue Ridge Assembly, near the
             location of Black Mountain, on a land owned by the YMCA, Young Men's Christian Association163, founded by Dr.
             Willis Duke Weatherford, director of the YMCA del Sur, in 1906. When it was installed before 1941, it was then
             transferred to the owners for the full use of the installations164. In August 1933, Rice signed a contract to fill the
             classes in September 165 in Blue Ridge Assembly, a private property district conforming to several buildings, located
             in Robert E. Hall and the main hall, built in 1911-12 by architect Louis Jallade . The central part of the organization is
             designed for these buildings, if the Robert E. Hall building is larger and larger than the previous one, and therefore it
             is organized by other volumes that generate a large interior space for access to the various buildings. These are the
             gymnasium (1915), Asheville Hall (1926), Abbott Hall (1927), College Hall (1918), Craft and Child Care Center (1925)
             and Martha Washington Residence (1914), which can be seen in the adjacent plan , Figure 1.37, junto al resto de
             edificaciones de menor escala y uso, construidos entre 1913-1927.




             The Robert E. Hall, currently named the Eureka building, is a three-story construction with a U-shaped structure and
             a large exterior portico. It is organized around a central area, the vestibule, where you have the side aisles and where
             you leave the dormitories, aulas and community life spaces of the college. In the lower plant there are administrative
             spaces, libraries, reading rooms, teachers' studios and teachers, although the second plant is located in the studio
             and dormitory spaces of women and in the superior plant the study and dormitory spaces of the women hombres166.
             The space destined for the individual study within the community of BMC on top of the campus will be one of the
             most significant aspects of the college. A private and individual space for concentration and work for each student,
             while the dormitory was shared.




             In this step, the teachers, in reduced groups, are organized spatially according to the methodology and educational
             content. Through the images we observe examples of master classes in a space where the teacher is located next to
             the floor and the students take notes and work in organized groups in threads, which alternate with more informal
             organizations where the physical proximity is essential for communication. The physical limits of the lecture in the
             room are loosened, expanding the space of the vestibule and the porch into a community environment that facilitates
             debates, lectures and conferences. The large vestibule and the exterior porch provide the main scenarios for
             academic activities, social meetings, conferences and meetings between others. The vestibule functions as a large
             living room, with furnishings and chimneys, so you can meet to talk and share classes in reduced groups. The porch,
             of great altitude and con

                  The origin and the existence of the BMC are connected to the legend of the Modern
                  Movement in the USA. Through European architects, designers and artists who were
                  forced to exile from Europe. In these moments, a year before that college
                  at its doors, the MoMA, through the exhibition, Modern Architecture: International
                  Exhibition1 and the publication of The International Style, written by Henry-Russell
                  Hitchcock and Philip Johnson, in 1932, presented to the great public the modern
                  European and North American architecture , including testimonials from examples
                  carried out in North America2 . Philip Johnson, como director of the Department of
                  Architecture of MoMA between 1930-36 and 1946-54, publica Built to live in3
                                               , edited by the museum in 1931, in which it was manifested,
                  declared the need to showcase modern architecture in the USA. for its capacity to
                  shelter new routes. We present a revolutionary architecture based on the exploitation
                  of the possibilities of the new materials, steel and hormigón armado, which is
                  available to the architects. The text states that modern architecture is based on the
                  project that meets the requirements of the program and goes beyond the design of
                  the building as a simple problem.
                                                                           , consider that it is presented
                  as “a new style that adapts to any type of structure, whether it is for industrial,

                  religious or residential use”                . Otherwise, express the necessity of
                  realize an architectural exhibition in the museum, which has not been included in
                  this discipline within the exhibition program. In this case, there is an outline of the
                  content and structure of the exhibition that is exhibited a year later and that is
                  completed with the publication of the book in which modern architecture unfolds as
                  a style of unitary character that is widely distributed following three composing
                  principles: architecture like volume, regularity and ausencia decoración6 .



                  Alongside these denominations, the architecture of the Modern Movement has

                   2.1 LEARNING ARQUITECTURA AND LEARNING SPACES



                  Before the pregunta of how to learn architecture, there is no immediate response nor
                  much easier. The learning of architecture occurs, not exclusively of personal vocation,
                  but is a process based on particular experiences that allow one of the students to learn
                  progressively, making it possible for individual consolidation within the schools10. Algo
                  que allos as docentes y antiguos o eternos students de arquitectura conocemos, es la
                  associación, en manyos casos dudosa, entre aprobar un exam, práctica o proyecto
                  academico, con la evidencia de conocimientos adquiridos. This is our way to (re)plant
                  if there are better ways of learning and studying and, consequently, new and better
                  ways of teaching. This line is based on learning about architecture in the BMC, based
                  on interactive and experiential learning. A method that allows critical thinking, the
                  communication of ideas, the resolution of problems through creative pursuits and self-
                  learning results vital in the process, in an effort to integrate distinct areas of knowledge
                  of transversal ways. This eternal cue is about how the plant and the future of
                  architectural schools are presented, about the transformation of educational projects
                  and what they consist of as architects. This controversy is closely linked to the reflection
                  of the fact that he is a professor of architecture and that in this chapter he addresses
                  the work carried out by the students of the college.




                  In the BMC the presence of the architecture is constant, through a proposed activity of
                  plants that on multiple occasions llegan to materialize and that form part, not only of its
                  program formativo, but also of the property construction of the mismo. The architecture
                  in this case, goes directly to the practice establishing a direct relationship between
                  project and construction.
                  Therefore, all of them can be completed and adapted to the campus depending on the
                  needs and collective interests. Aside from the fact that the total competency of the title
                  is never offered, the architecture is understood in the educational process within the
                  context of the arts, but also as an argument in which the community functions as a
                  client in the decision-making process, adecuándose a the reality, applying the acquired
                  knowledge and with a compromise on our actions. This also manifests itself from the
                  notion that redefines the conditions of experimentation of space not of individual nature,
                  if not as part of a community11.



                  The architecture played a major role in the college 's curriculum from the start of the
                  Land Market, in 1937, on the new campus at Lake Eden. The construction of the
                  campus property constitutes not only the consolidation of the physical body of the BMC,
                  but also of the intellectual, which leaves the educational project of collective management
                  and a key to experimentation. In the central stage, while architecture has a relevant
                  paper in the educational curriculum, it is understood as part of the educational
                  community and the social group, in this way

                  During the 1946 verano, architect Harry Seidler participated in the verano courses
                  organized and gave conferences19 to reflect on the teaching of architecture. A teaching
                  that, traditionally, is based on the study of art history and architecture as a direct
                  application of creative processes, of carbon drawing, of imitation of human proportions
                  and of adopting the appropriate style. subsequently enter the program. A system for
                  reproducing and copying projects published in reviews. Prior to this reality, Seidler
                  demonstrated that creativity cannot be achieved, but must be encouraged by the
                  emotion that the future architecture owes to express itself. This requires instruction in
                  basic design, free composition, experimentation with materials, color, space and
                  scientific study of optical illusions. Apuesta por el replacement del bujo en dos
                  dimensionse y las perspectivas de bujos y visualization en 3D, así como la construcción
                  de models para el studio y la presentationación de propuestas. All this is to err on the
                  side of eclecticism, but also to eliminate the rapid expansion of modern architecture
                  degraded to a single style with multiple clichés. In this line, Gropius, in his article Living
                  Architecture or International Style?20, reveals the abandonment of the tendency to
                  reproduction and imitation as media for the acquisition of knowledge and apuesta for
