                   2.2 PROGRAM DOCENTE ARCHITECTURA EN EL BMC



                  The BMC does not offer the full competence of the architectural title. The program that
                  was offered was parcial and its method did not cover all technical aspects, but it
                  supported one of the points of participation in the progressive education of contemporary
                  architecture. There is no such school of architecture as there is, therefore, no
                  qualification for training. Sin embargo, hay confirmed that, in 1941, Dreier had the
                  opportunity to form an architectural school, in the sense of a complete offer of theoretical
                  and practical knowledge that was articulated by Albers and Kocher fundamentally, with
                  the necessity of contracting other professors, to complete the offer and to form the
                  educational project.27 A weight of the past, it is possible to consider that it is possible
                  to prepare the student for its architecture.



                  The distinguished professors who participated in the teaching were the ones who
                  formed and contained the classes and the understanding of what was supposed to be
                  architectural. We transmit knowledge and thought, with the object of which the student
                  discovers the architecture in all its dimensions and in all its complexity. Gropius, who
                  maintains a strong relationship with the BMC that continues for many years, affirms that
                  the teacher must take into account the reality of the object and do not project the subject
                  of the student, apostando por una creativa en la educación , stimulated by learning by
                  making and doing to generate and enhance culture. Recapacitaba que el excessivo
                  enfasis en la educación por el conocimiento de los libros no es capaz de generar las
                  condiciones necesarias para el aprendizaje, por lo que recomendaba, para empezar, el
                  vaciar la mente. Therefore, the first object and problem of the design teacher was to
                  liberate the intellectual frustration of the student, animándole to confide in his
                  subconscious and intend to recover the receptivity of his mind without any type of
                  prejudice inherited from childhood. Se trataría de avoid recaer en la imitation y basarnos
                  en nuestra propia experiencia y observation28.




                  To analyze the presence of the architecture in the BMC, we can differentiate between
                  different brands by the developments that follow in the community and by the personal
                  initiatives taken from the program. An initial story by Albers and Schawinsky from
                  another central stage, of greater intensity and speech by Kocher, which included from
                  1939 to 1943. Finally, a recent stage, from 1949, received a large number of visits of
                  architects. During the last years of its existence, the teaching of architecture is already
                  organized, but it is self-managed and presents its proposed projects, with little weight
                  and relevance in the overall curriculum of the BMC.

                   1st STEP:
                   THE ARQUITECTURA IN THE PRIMEROS AÑOS
                   In the initial stage of the BMC the presence of the architecture that was reduced to the concepts worked
                   by Albers and Schawinsky, who approached the principles and reflections on the architectural concepts
                   in their classes, clearly influenced by the Bauhaus from which they came. Ambos comes to the college
                   with the opportunity to continue with the beginning allí, convirtiendo sus classes, en cierta manera, en
                   un curso preparatorio para students de arquitectura.



                  Josef Albers is a clear motor of BMC's artistic activity. Sus classes se
                   You can start in your Preliminary Course or Vorkurs, although it is also in the Bauhaus and there are
                  very classes. A first drawing course, Basic Drawing, aims to unlock the ability to draw across the vista.
                   Consisting of exercises in which the form was worked through the observation and its transcription into
                   the space. Para ello, an ejercicio que plantaba initially era la escritura en espejo o que scribes con su
                   mano non dominant, con el objectivivo de desafiar sus ábitos y empezar aeducar su vista y
                   observación29. An object that was included in the BMC, “I want to open eyes”30 and that was converted
                   into a college letter.




                   In addition to the classes of Werklerhre31, we introduced students with prior experience in the drawing,
                   Albers faced the design of the unlimited possibilities of the materials and with the objectives of
                   understanding its naturalness and developing the concept of space. For the difference between the
                   components that were named
                   Material and Material32. In Matter we explored the appearance of materials, analyzing their
                   characterization through visual and tactile perception. The difference between structure (the
                   characteristics of the surface area of the primary material), factura (characteristics that show how it has
                   been technically processed by the material) and texture, which has reference to the structure and the
                  factura if it is daban in the material simultaneously33. Therefore, in Material it works with the possibilities
                   and capacities of the material in function of its naturalness. Seen fundamentally in the multiple ways of
                   experimenting with the properties of the paper through its handling.




                   According to the same method, it is assigned to the class of color theory, Color Course34, as a technical
                  preparation for painting and for a “disciplined use of color”35.

                  the creative possibilities of the scenario, organize ourselves into groups to represent
                  our propias ideas at the company's restaurant, so that we have some time for
                  reflection and preparation. After all representations, we discuss with the group the
                  validation of the message that you request to transmit with each of our interventions,
                  with the possibility of repeating and improving them in case it is necessary for the
                  suggestions of the group restaurant. For these interventions we use lighting effects,
                  attractive elements, clothing and inexpressive white masks, to focus attention on
                  physical expression and movement. We deal with solutions that we have worked in
                  relation to concepts such as form, space, color, optics, sound, language, mathematics,
                  music, rhythm, poetry, space, colors, construction, architecture, materials and illusion.

                   must be validated at the rest of the class. Punctually, also professors from other
                   areas were invited to provide an introduction on topics related to the representations.

                   2ªETAPA:
                   THE ARCHITECTURE AND CONSTRUCTION AS THE IMPULSE OF THE COLLEGE MODEL The architecture

                   appears as it was signed in the course 1941-42, advertising it as an area of knowledge that requires creative skills,
                   technical knowledge and experience. Una training, the architecture, which needs to be updated with the latest constructive
                   techniques39. The BMC architectural student received training based on the knowledge gained from the development of
                   the design by the author and complemented with the real practice of science, as the key to his creative growth. Some

                   knowledge and basic cultural preparation, which starts from the study of social sciences and physics, as well as knowledge
                   of history and literature. In the central stage of the architectural teaching, under the direction of Kocher40 and assisted by
                   the architects Howard Dearstyne and Anatole Kopp, it was supported by the design as its propedéutica for planning and
                   subsequently, applied to the construction. We are dedicated to investigating and debating the necessary programs for each
                   project and complementing it with real construction through practical training. In this way, the value of the coordination of
                   the creative work and the project activity, with the materialization of the proposals, lies in the effort to understand the
                   realities of the construction and the real experience with low professional supervision.




                   Kocher's educational program includes several theoretical signatures that include the aspects
                   considered necessary for architectural training: design assignments, structural calculations,
                   history and construction. In Architectural Elements, the course offers to investigate the form
                  and materials of laboratory methodology, carrying out projects to determine the program of
                   requirements and relate to the necessary space and optimally to uncover the activities and
                   relationships. In Intermediate Design, we work with the user and program in the architecture,
                   using very dimensional designs and models. In the Advanced Design course, there are major
                   scale and expansion jobs, including urban planning topics. These assignments are complete

                  The Work Program, a job program, supplements the training requirements of the college
                  requirements, including the economy, but responds to its ideal principles. We are willing
                  to expand the personal experience of each student's work with the community, learning
                  different offices and manual jobs, as well as the management and organization of group
                  activities. It is offered to all students, and not only to those interested in architecture, but
                  also to the idea of being involved in all of the institutions. So many women like men
                  participate in voluntary work, among the classes, in the maintenance of the community,
                  without interfering with academic standards. This program is directly related to
                  architecture and construction, but also with areas such as economics and sociology.42




                  Its origin goes back to when the BMC was located in Blue Ridge Assembly and you had
                  to prepare the ground for the new installations of Lake Eden that were suitable for its
                  academic use. The construction of the studio building by the students and professors
                  was due to the materialization and practicality of the objects and valued plants. Once
                  installed in September 1941, the community aspects acquired greater importance and
                  with the Work Program. All of these tasks are intended to ensure that the BMC is self-
                  sustaining.43 However, the need for the program is not the sole reason for its existence,
                  but it is only the idea of breaking the limit that is established by the aulas and expands
                  it at the same time. For another reason, the idea of interrelation between physical and
                  intellectual work for the realization of the persona and which one of them assumes is

                   The camp is organized by 30 campers, women and men, and even as in the general

                   per week, as long as they are free for all periods and some delays. During free time
                   we organize cultural activities, fiestas, dances and excursions in the area.



                  These documents and files65 are considered essential training in wartime where
                  you can learn skills and practical skills before a possible emergence. Learn the
                  limitations and leys of each material with the way it is processed, understood and
                  implemented in practice with the various constructive and applicable technologies.
                  Additionally, the first contact with modern architecture resulted66. The Work Camp,
                  in its final stage, is centered on the development of the granja67 and the grounds of
                  the college, where it works for 5 hours on a daily basis and is complemented by the
                  rest of the activities, classes and sizes that are offered in the music institutes It is
                  here that we offer all types of students, constituting one of the main source of
                  ingredients.

                  These camps also serve as an advertising service to be able to train and to support
                  new alumni during the academic course, which can be advertised and supported in
                  addition to the art and music courses that they also enjoy during the summer period.
                  The work camps extend to all of us as a democratic response to the needs of citizens
                  in times of war and an approximation of practical experience.

                   The first of them, Work Camp for Democracy, arose in 1938 in West Park, New
                   York. From now on, everything will be managed by a national committee that
                   supervises these programs.

                   Rudofsky, who completes the architectural program offering, is at this moment
                   without the presence of a resident architect. During these years, in the verano
                   courses, a total of 8.5 h / week is intended for the classes of architecture (5.5 h /

                  In the case of Sert, who previously visited the college in 1940, he viewed the verano
                  of 1944 to give a taller view of the urban plane specifically for the visualization of 84 ,
                  ideas in the modern plane through collections such as models, photographs and
                  films, among others, that allows to promote urbanism. This discussion is based on
                  the collaboration between urbanists, economists, sociologists, historians and other
                  specialists who, as consultants, can help establish the necessary programs.

                  For another part, this team works together with painters, artists and photographers
                  at the time of producing models. An organized taller titled Modern City and Regional
                  Planning85, which includes three conferences: The Human Scale in Planning,
                  Urbanism versus Suburbanism and Civic-center Tomorrow and which is
                  complemented by a practical part which materializes our ideas on urbanism through
                  models to scale as herramienta to visualize in very dimensions the problems involved
                  in the modern plane. The course is organized in three parts. In a first section we
                  establish a theoretical basis on the main ideas of modern urbanism, adelantándose
                  y estableciendo las necesidades en el tiempo de la posguerra. For example, we
                  propose to establish a program for a city in the USA of 100,000 inhabitants, for a
                  community in Brazil of 50,000 inhabitants and the reconstruction of a central
                  European city of 50,000 inhabitants. The part of the taller, of greatest importance for
                  Service, is centered on the exploration of communication routes and visualization of
                  established and detected problems in the first phase, which allows you to generate
                  opinions and debates on ideas that, if you are interested in other materials will not
                  be produced. Finally, in the third section it focuses on the dissemination of these
                  ideas of modern urbanism, so that it specifies the collaboration of students, professors
                  and periodists, among others.




                  Just in 1944, Rudofsky planted a taller design, organized together with her woman
                  Berta, called Clothing: Present, Past and Future86.
                  In fact, from the proximity to the clothing and the shoe, we plant it in our new behavior
                  and in the different aspects of our life, we eat the history of fashion throughout the
                  length of our civilizations and with a practical part of which it is a good idea. A critical
                  review of student clothing. Then you should self-examine your clothing form while
                  designing, preparing designs and products, and then exploring the relationships
                  between the skin, the clothes and the space.

                   2.3 WHO´S WHO. ARQUITECTOS Y OTROS PROFESIONALES RELACIONADOS CON LA
                   ARQUITECTURA VINCULADOS AL BMC




                   The BMC received visits from professionals from all areas of knowledge, such as Carl Jung, Albert
                   Einstein, Fernand Léger or Aldous Huxley, among others. Many of them are those who have left their
                   background or my college background due to their professional experience and their personal personality.
                   However, by the achievements of diverse natural experiences in which the college was the scene of its
                   birth, such as the first happening in a cargo by John Cage, the creation of the dance company of
                   Cunnigham or the experiments of Fuller with our alumni, that The speaker has revealed the concept of
                   tension, to cite some of the most popular ones. La historia de la institución ha sido narrada desde el eco
                   mediático de muchas de estas figuras del arte y de la literatura principalmente.




                  Sin embargo, en el campo de la architecura, su diffusion ha sido mucho menor y trivial.




                   With the aim of being able to discover which people propelled the architecture and were able to evaluate
                   the presence of the discipline, it was established and ordered, chronologically, the distinguished
                   professionals who were involved in the educational project. Experts that establish the degree of distinction
                   with the BMC, depending on their estancias and that it is plasmad in a diagram that allows reading
                   through the time, detecting something that will significantly collaborate in the teaching of architecture. We
                   proceed to a classification based on the duration of your relationship with the project, organized into four
                   categories based on the following temporal ranges:




                             -Visits, as a short one-time establishment, in a master class mode or approximation to design
                             the educational project, -Cursos de verano de uno a dos meses
                             de racción aproximada. It officially opened as the Art Summer Institute from 1944 to 1953


                             On the Lago campus, with previous experience, it is organized in minor and exclusive courses
                             for those enrolled in the college. These are fundamental results, which can support the view of
                             the BMC to our new teachers and students, -Media estancia that is equivalent to semester
                             courses and -Larga estancia
                             that corresponds to complete annual academic courses.




                   The steps that are established in the architectural educational program, in relation to the intensity of your
                   presence and participation in the academic life, can be translated to determine the roles of the figures
                   that participate in the project. So we can differentiate a first step, in the beginning, with a major presence
                   of architects that will increase until 1940.


                   Another step, until the year 1944, when the architecture acquired the paper relevant to the idea with the
                   impulse of the Work Program, which then continued until 1949, when the architecture was present, but
                   the number of professional vinculados came down in the period of posguerra.

                   LOS STUDENTS OF ARQUITECTURA
                  If we hear the BMC as a place where the teaching is done in community and where the convivencia is a
                   common question to solve the problems, this is a unique condition for the activity of our students. We are
                   not the only ones who attend the classes and carry out their work, otherwise they are involved in training
                   with a compromised and proposed activity, as they show that the continuation is signaled and included in
                   the diagram and list.




                  It deals with a way of thinking that we put into practice the theoretical knowledge we have acquired, and
                   it was announced in the 1943 advertisement for the course for students of architecture and interested in
                   construction: "The student who receives training in architecture requires
                                           experience practice in the construction field to complement the design and technical
                                       training of the classes. The idea of the construction field work is a corollary of the
                                       reflexive concept of understanding the architect as 'constructor'. consider it essential
                                           for the full opening of the student. In this time of war, this experience offers
                                       opportunities to discover engineering, practical skills and ability to meet certain types
                                           of emergency. The students learn that the materials have limitations and their propias;
                                           and that working with them requires discipline and technology. Some students in the
                                       Work Camp have a high degree of skill in the various types of defects they are
                                       involved in; y para la mayoría de los studientes resulta valiosa la experiencia de




                                       consider first hand modern architecture and materials”.151

                  With a tray in the BMC similar to the previous one we met Claude Stoller. On his
                  estate between 1939 and 1943 he focused on art and architecture studios, being
                  alumni of Albers, Kocher, Dearstyne and Voight. Take the BMC with the
                  recommendation of his artist Ezra Stoller, an architectural photographer who, among
                  other works, photographed the models of Gropius and Breuer for Lago Eden. As a
                  student built the farm and, together with his colleague Charles Forberg, directed the
                  work, in the verano of 1941, of the life of the Jalowetz family, ambos projects
                  designed by Kocher. La Jalowetz House is a living room built with prefabricated
                  materials whose acoustics are decisive, because the inhabitant is a musical pianist,
                  resident music teacher. Therefore, it is revisited with acoustic technology and
                  perforated wood. You have participated in the Housing Committee, moderated by
                  Anni Albers, which is responsible for the organization and management of
                  accommodations for teachers and students164.



                  Tras su estancia en el college se alistó en el ejército y, tras la guerra, contactó del
                  nuevo con el BMC to return to our architectural studies165. Sin embargo, united in
                  1946 with the Harvard Graduate School of Design, together with Don Page,
                  Alexander Reed and Charles Forberg, graduated in 1949. Stoller opened a large
                  professional tray as an architect founding his studio proprietor, first Marquis & Stoller,
                  in San Francisco en 1956 y después Stoller Partners, en Berkeley en 1978. Esto lo
                  ha chocho paginándolo con la docencia en la Universidad de California y la de
                  Berkeley, donde reside en la actualidad.

                  In 1944, Charles Forberg, a Kocher alumnus, led the expansion of the South Lodge
                  accommodations, planned by Mc Guire Wood. The building was one of the existing
                  constructions on the campus when it was mudared and used as a male residence.
                  A block of back plants that, with the amplification, increased its capacity to
                  accommodate the kitchens of the college, the house was set on fire. On the other
                  hand, he collaborated in the creation of scenes for multiple representations like
                  Outward Bound, by Sutton Vane, in April 1944 and co-directed with Stoller the life of
                  the Jalowetz. In 1945, the BMC was installed to help prepare the land where
                  prefabricated buildings were located, solicited by the FHA, Federal Housing
                  Authority166.


                  From among our students who came directly or indirectly to the architectural
                  discipline, we also encountered the case of Donald Wight. It was a school that was
                  born in 1946 with the proposal to host a debate on the way of life at the college,
                  before the project was carried out by TAC, specifically by Norman Fletcher, as
                  representative of the architectural team. Faced with the proposal for a large building
                  of dormitories with dormitory and combined studies, Wight, with other students,
                  prepared an alternative project of small residential units, proposing a collective
                  dormitory and private individual studies167. This initiative generated a great
                  controversy in the community that lasted for more than a year, but ultimately it never
                  happened.

                   2.4 ARCHITECTURAL PRODUCTION IN THE ARCHITECTURAL PROGRAM



                  As the result of the architectural program which combines the preparatory classes of
                  design funds, provided by Albers, with the theoretical classes and the construction
                  experience, the Lago Eden campus is converted into a territory of experimentation in
                  which it stands out. Buildings in a natural environment. This triad represents the ideal
                  program that Gropius had the taste of to translate to Harvard and where there is direct
                  contact with the materials and technologies within and where to go from there.



                   As you can see, Albers's design and color courses claim to “shelter the eyes” to discover
                   the naturalness of the forms, work on the relative appearance of the materials, as well
                   as their properties, result in the essentials of the training architecture and there is a
                   demand for students interested in architecture. The other focus of attraction is the Work
                   Program, from the moment when it expands in the range of intervention during
                   construction, as well as the basic defects in the maintenance of the community, from the
                   beginning of 1940.



                  We consider that the architecture courses that we provide do not provide the training expected to
                   graduate, the participation of students in the constructive process may be considered exceptional, for
                   the number of interested parties and for their degree of involvement1 . The fundamental aspect of the
                   Work Program is the possibility of intensive inclusion in all phases of the constructive process and the
                   establishment of an excellent learning relationship between professor and alumni. However, the
                   significant contribution is the direct relationship between the design process, the project and its
                   materialization, but all of the actual results of the interventions.




                   In this step, he directed the tasks that were developed with a coherence based on
                   necessity and a common strategy for developing the campus. On the other hand, during
                   the war and the march of the first, those who performed their sporadic and autonomous
                   work, due to the non-existence of a clear direction of the program, did not lose its
                   experimental character. All the parts, including the ones that we are going to build, form
                   part of the formatting process. Example of this is the unrealized project of Gropius and
                   Breuer, which continues to be explained. The college participated in its program of
                   necessities and in the debates on the way of living in community. And as we see it, we
                   need to hear the feedback launched by some students who had the project presented
                   by TAC and that sparked a debate where it was attached to the architecture in a first
                   plan. Questions that will be addressed in the chapter of the observations.

                   THE DISENCADENT
                   In one way, the objects built within the Work Program have their origins in the project
                   developed by Gropius and Breuer that, with an ultimately unviable result, resulted in the
                   decline of all subsequent architectural production on the campus.



                   In the loading of the project for the new installations in the Lake Eden2       , Albers and
                   Kocher actúan de mediadores apostando por los dos primeros, como arquitectos con
                   experiencia y representatives de la arquitectura moderna, para realizar un designe
                   simple y práctico, que es lo que el college necesitaba.3 Esta propuesta fue llevada a
                   votación, como era habitual en la toma de decisions, for the diversity of opinions existing
                   between the members of the BMC. Ante la postura de Albers, que defendía la
                   professionalidad y experiencia de Gropius frente a other arquitectos jóvenes con ganas
                   de conseguir un lugar en la arquitectura, otros miembros, en cambio, temían el coste y
                   escala del proyecto de los europeos y preferían arquitecto más joven.



                  In 1939 they were loaded with the project4 , which was released a long time ago after
                  negotiations on the program, the situation and the honoraries5 and with the idea that
                  the new architecture represented the ideals of the college. The process generated a
                  debate on the needs of the community, the educational principles and the management
                  in which the architecture was the physical support of the BMC query. One of the
                  fundamental premises was the separation of dormitories from work and study spaces,
                  for economic reasons and ideas for education6 . The position of the college prioritizes
                  the construction of the study areas, related to the accommodations, which can be
                  established in the preexistences of the campus, as it is. Furthermore, as a co-educational
                  center, I prefer to separate the estancias from men and women with privacy.



                  A finals of this year, Gropius regresa to explain the propuesta. An ambitious project so
                  much for the program as for the scale, for around 250 alumni and a surface area of
                  approximately 41,600m27 . A complete set of volumes connected by past video clips,
                  “avoid one

                   The first phase, edificio A, was a building of five plants with aulas, spaces for relationships and work, 120

                  as the Lee Hall building, without notice from the BMC. The second floor, buildings B and C, provide

                   third floor and final stage include the auditorio and dining room (edificio E) for several representations with
                   a capacity of 450 people, the science hall (edificio D) and the rest of the buildings (F,G,H,I yJ ).




                   During the first year of 1940, the BMC started the financing campaign to raise the first $75,000 of the
                   $500,000 estimated for the construction of the educational complex. You will be able to communicate with
                   your objects through various media prints, so that you do not need to disseminate the project.


                   National and local journals and magazines, such as Life Magazine, The New York Times, New York Herald
                   Tribune and Architectural Forum, among others, publish the project and the financing campaign that is in
                   progress. These actions must be removed from the exhibition of the plan, with all the documentation of the
                   first making and the model in the MoMA, in 1940, which is accompanied by a charla a cargo of the
                   architects. This action will have to be repeated in June of this year.




                   However, the media environment and the fields will result in inadequacy11 for the viability and scale of the
                  project. Before the economic difficulties to get to the country, see Gropius, in one of his visits, a new project
                   with minimal constructive requirements in the case of the use of materials12, which is not carried out.
                   Finally, in the verano of 1940 the project was abandoned. The entry into the war affects this impossibility,
                  so disregard the intentions of possible donors, be concerned with other restrictions and restrictions on
                   construction materials. Other than the reasons why we do not have full financing, it is difficult to agree with
                   the donors, because many of them are required as the BMC is a more conventional educational model,
                   despite the reputation that maintains a radical institution and not accredited, that does not guarantee the
                   stability that you request them. From the beginning to the Cape, this is the first important example of
                   European architects in the USA.

                  ADEQUACY OF PREEXISTENCES


                   Interventions carried out under preexisting conditions are mainly based on improving the thermal
                   conditions for heating and reforming the interior distribution to create new bedrooms. It must be noted
                   that the Lago campus was a vacation retreat with buildings of various scales and capacity for up to 150
                   veraneantes, intended for the enjoyment of the natural setting, near the Blue Ridge Parkway13.




                   A tourist complex built by EW Grove, entrepreneur, promoter and constructor with properties in the
                   Asheville and nearby areas. It consists of 14 buildings built fundamentally in wood and stone, which the
                   new property, el BMC, adapted mainly to accommodate the families of the children.

                  NEW PLANT CONSTRUCCIONES

                   The various constructions that arose at the college, through the Work Program, took place in a temporal
                   period that began before the mudanza in 1939 and continued until 1953, four years before the definitive
                   construction of the BMC.




                  <NEW COTTAGE or WOOD´S COTTAGE_
                   1939> Designed by Josef Albers and built by local residents of Black Mountain, with several
                   students, it was the first of its new plants on campus, in the first of 1939. A life of the back homes
                   with a bathroom, kitchen and a living room with a fireplace intended to accommodate the family
                   responsible for the maintenance of the farm, the Mac Woods. Subsequently it was used by the
                   Moles family, working at the farm and by the family of Max Dehn23. In 2007 it was remodeled,
                  adding a volume.




                  <STUDIES BUILDING_1940-41>
                   The Studies Building constitutes the largest scale project built within the program and, moreover, is at
                   the critical moment when the EU is going to participate in the European conflict. Following the shipment
                   in July 1940, Kocher accepted and agreed to be an invited professor and established himself in the BMC
                   during the construction period of the project.




                   In the program of initial requirements, which the college offers to architects24 , solicits 80 individual    THE



                   studies for students, 20 studies for personal, which can be used as a reduced size, 3 suites for teachers
                   (one by one), library with office and despacho, reading room, meeting room with chimney, 7 offices
                   (rector, secretary, registrar, assistant, assistant and 3 administrators), despacho or reception room for

                   for plant.

                   Williams and her mother, and returned as teaching by the BMC. The property was demolished in
                   2007 by the property.

                  <EDIFICIOS FEDERAL HOUSING AUTHORITY, FHA_1946-47>
                  This classification of new constructions must include a series of prefabricated buildings that will be
                   constructed, but not designed or facilitated, with state subsidy, to solve the housing problems.




                  Throughout the World War II there has been a considerable increase in the number of students, many of
                   them veterans of the war, who were supported by the GI Bill42 program. This is a problem due to the
                   difficulty in keeping all of them in undesirable conditions. Ante los apuros económicos, solicitan ayudas
                   ayudas al FHA y a la Public Work Agency, which offered economical solutions a modo de barracones
                   militares, que eran facilitados a las universidades que los quequerían.




                  Considering the approval of the solicitud in the first instance of 194643, the llegada del cuando can be
                  material has deteriorated until the end of the year , constructed and used
                   Los four primeros barracones.

                   couples. Each of our accommodations has 3 apartments with living room, bedroom, bathroom and
                   kitchen. Other buildings that were built were used as a building, with students and students. It was known
                   as the number of The Eye, and also referred to as The I. The last one, the Deep South, was destined for
                   a male dormitory.




                   A year after the Public Work Agency facilitated other three prefabricated buildings, which were used as a
                   studio building, called The Stable, as a library and almacen. Prefabricated wooden buildings must be

                                         “Also you know the connection is also a point of encuentro, a
                                         connection in which the lines of unlimited recorrido are intersected
                                         with other creando nodal points of an emerging intensity. Finally the
                                         recognition is an understanding, the result of the action of a subject
                                         that in the flow of these reactions catches more than the attention or
                                         more the knowledge to retain them. It is a subjective action. Produce
                                         a moment of joy and fragile fullness.”1




                  If we understand the realization as the result of the action, we can have a translation
                  to the architecture, where we see what is being built and we can design it as an activity
                  that is being built in time and space. It takes the form of thinking in architecture as a
                  system of solutions generated by actions and realizations of various and abiertas.



                  Según Gilles Deleuze, los acontecimientos se distinguishedn por que ser discernibles,
                  “apparente siempre por contrast con un ruido de fondo”2 y por su bifocalidad, se
                  definen porque su realización tiene lugar en el orden de la naturaleza y en el del
                  lenguaje3 , guardando entre This is a complementary relationship. As soon as you
                  know, there is no sufficient signal to indicate what is happening to you, otherwise it will
                  also indicate the circumstances in which the accident occurs and the convergence of
                  the sequences of the predictions that are possible for you to realize. Also explains that
                                                                                              4
                  the problem is defined by its “problem mode”                                  , concerns
                   exclusively the problems and describes these conditions. This acknowledges that there are some global
                  effects that affect the compression in the present, but also that it holds in the past and in the future. Finally,
                  the statement became concrete in its singularity: “It is a group of singular points that characterize a
                   mathematical curve, a state of physical affairs, a psychological and moral person. Its points of retroceso, of
                   inflection, etc.; collados, nudos, focos, centros; points of melting, condensing, boiling, etc.; points of sadness
                   and joy, of enfermedad and of salvation, of hope and of angustia, points of sensitive llamados”5 . These four
                   results can be appreciated in the creative processes. In this way, it is possible to think that all of the
                   achievements contain an essential element of creativity that I take with artistic production, establishing
                   between the different fields of knowledge a system of communicating vessels to support the production of
                   achievements .

                   Within this correlation between art and accompaniments, Harold Rosenberg recognized
                   the term Action Painting in his essay The American Action Painters of 1952, which
                   demonstrated the importance of action in the link as its propia representation. Gestures
                   for the liberation of political, aesthetic and moral values that are carried out and
                   supported. The art heard as an action that descansa sobre el supuesto de que el artista
                   acepta como real lo que ocurre pendante su processo creativo, poniendo énfasis en la
                   experiencia o experimentación de los nuevos action painters y donde l’spectador se
                   introduce dans la action. This way, where there is a link, it is not an image but there is
                   an acknowledgment.
                  According to the text:


                                            “As soon as the pintor turned into an actor, the viewer needed to think
                                            about a vocabulary relating to the action: its inception, its durability, its
                                            psychological state, its concentration and its release of voluntad, its
                                            pasivity, its hope in alert. It must be converted into a recognizer of the
                                            gradations between the automatic, the spontaneous, the evocado.”
                                                                                    7




                   These definitions are close to the understanding of the successful constructions in the
                   BMC, which determine the capacity of the architecture to be implemented, to exist
                   without a finality nor concrete durability. An interest lies in the qué and how it happens,
                   in the process, more than in a material result
                   concrete.

                   3.1 ACCOUNTS AND ICONIC IMAGES OF BMC



                  The history of the BMC has often been represented by the singularity of people,
                  some professors such as alumni, who participated in the project or the artistic and
                  cultural legacy of distinguished authors. Without embargo, it also reveals the truth
                  that we have through the instantaneous distinctions that have been preserved.
                  Images that reflect only the people that form part of it, but also the atmosphere, the
                  actions, the environments, the places, the architecture, the situations and the
                  developments that follow the length of their lives and four years in which This is in
                  operation. Fracciones of time immortalized by several images that were taken from
                  the photo, as indicated by Susan Sontag9 , then transformed into images that
                  followed the same image as the one that appeared in the imagen10. Our perception
                  of the situations is articulated by the interventions of the camera11 which immortalize
                  these moments and which invite them into

                   accounts.

                  Instantly recorded by large photos that participated as professors in various current
                  courses, such as Hazel Larsen Archer, (who was also a BMC alumna), Josef
                  Breitenbach, Harry Callahan, Fritz Goro, Robert Hass, Barbara Morgan, Beaumont
                  Newhall, Aaron Siskind y Arthur Siegel. Also by visitors like Clemens Kalicher, Helen
                  M. Post or Edward Steichen. All of them immortalized moments, people and
                  scenarios, as much of the BMC as their heroes, from an artistic approximation. For
                  other students, such as John Campbell, Nick Cernovich, Will Hamlin, Andy Oates,
                  Vernon Philips, Robert Rauscheberg, John Stix, Stan Vanderbeek and Jonathan
                  Williams, we took photos in contemporary situations intermixed with our artistic
                  interests and included our images in a variety of ways. of the publications edited in
                  the BMC12. And yet, at the end of the 30s, the formation of a group of students
                  interested in photography, who included Claude Stoller and John Stix, and who
                  experimented with photography in the quarter of revelation13.




                   Many of the best moments recorded at the college were recorded by Albers and his
                   Leica camera, taken in images from common visual and visual diary situations, as
                   well as from the archives of our alumni's work. His interest in photography led him to
                   organize, in 1943, the conference Photos as Photography and Photos as Art14,
                   which manifested its way of hearing it.

                   3.2 ACCOUNTS OF OTRAS ARTES RELACIONADOS CON LA
                  ARCHITECTURE




                  Se han seleccionado una serie de contecimientos donde, a weighing que la architecture como disciplina
                   no estuvo present, permite understand el contexto globalo de lo sucedido en esta institución. Even after
                  the finals of our years we have reached the last stage of college life , when the architectural activity and,
                  therefore, the presence of architects in the BMC, has become decadent.


                  Sin embargo, en many of these selected contributions, the understanding and the manipulation of the
                   space resultan reveals in the way of designing the interdisciplinares collaborations. The actual selection
                   is made up of several staged representations of theater, music, dance and visual art that rise to the
                   bottom of various spatial organizations and a series of impulsive actors that appear in various variations
                   of them, as an example of the participatory spirit BMC.




                  OA01_THE DANCE MACABRE: A SOCIOLOGICAL STUDY (1938)
                   On May 14, 1938, this representation was directed by Schawinsky, where it was the last of its courses
                  as taught by the BMC and carried out with the collaboration of its alumni. The origin of the event was
                   found in the medieval text Dies Irae19, which was translated by students Martin Steneau and Robert
                   Sunley. An adaptation of the work to the final result with our alumni actors20 who represent the characters
                   of the Danse Macabre in a central scenario. This version of the object, which approaches the concept of
                   death to intend to encounter the absolute of our time21, is based on the effect of dress and design, has
                   collaborated with Don Page with its designs, and is accompanied by a set of lights Kenelm Winslow's
                   freighter sank there. The song included a musical piece composed by John Evarts, based on improvised
                   variations by Dies Irae and performed by the proprietor Evarts, Betty Sly, Peggy Barton, Leslie Katz,
                   Jane Mayhall and Allan Sly.




                  Through an academic course of work, essays, and designs, it is represented in the gymnasium22 of the
                  Blue Ridge, a usual scenario of Schawinsky's classes. The scene is organized around a central space
                   distributed between three circles of seats. The public assists us in the action we see on a journey made
                  up of one hat with black hoods and masks that were entered before the start of the journey, turning into
                  anonymous faces before death. The action is deactivated during repetitive movements and is symbolized
                   by funeral rituals, following the concept of the

                     Poema in medieval Latin, from the s. XIII, attributed to Tomás de Celano (1200-1260), some of which
                  are also possible authors of Papa Gregorio Magno, San Bernardo de Claraval or the fragile dominicos
                  Umbertus and Frangipani.

                  OA02_ GREEK PARTY(1946)
                  At the Summer Art Institute of 1946, the original artist Jean Varda was invited to share
                  painting classes with Jacob Lawrence. As the final brooch of the course, we organized,
                  a night out, a great temática fiesta en torno a la mitología griega, to prepare the classes
                  to be suspended during the days. A party where professors and students participated
                  in costumes of gray columns, the goddess Diana, the gods Mercurio, Cleopatra and
                  Diogenes, among others26. We took off at the dining room and, with the excuse of the
                  final celebration of the classes, we will build, in a collaborative manner, a large caballo
                  de Troya made of one of 12 pieces of viola (one of approximately 3.5m) of an excessive
                  height that is impeded. Pudiese meterse entro de l’espace de la fête. This structure, a
                  habitable structure, turned into a symbol of the work carried out between Varda and
                  her alumni.

                  OA03_THE RUSE OF MEDUSA (1948)
                   During the mismo verano that rose to the top of the supine dome, the intention was
                   to erect the geodesic cupola on the part of Buckminster Fuller, you saw the
                   representation of the obra The Ruse of Medusa, a theatrical function staged on the
                   night of the 14th of August 1948 and directed by John Cage, which formed part of
                   the concert program that he prepared at the college this year. Cage studied at the
                   BMC at the finals of the 30s and thought that it would be a good place to fund his
                   experimental music school27. Please note that we do not have to materialize any of
                   the projects, our projects in the BMC from these verano results in decisions in your
                   professional sector.

                  The course La Defense by Eric Satie was based in the studio of Satie's work, so that
                  he prepared the media concerts with a long duration of his work.
                  This concert cycle, offered at the college as an amateur music festival28, was
                  distributed in very few representations per week and was presented accompanied
                  by a previa explanation29. The Ruse of the Medusa was based on the 1913 album
                  The Trap of Medusa .

                  The original text of the article was translated into English by M. C. Richards30 and it
                  participated in a large part of the professors and alumni congregates this way. For
                  one part, John Cage31 as the organizador of the piano, the dancer and the
                  coreographer Merce Cunningham32 interpreted Jonás's paper with a single
                  mechanic, Buckminster Fuller represented the paper of Baron Medusa, Elaine De
                  Kooning played Frisette, the singer of Baron, Isaac Rosenfeld played by Polycarp,
                  William Shrauger appeared on the Astolto paper and Alvin Charles Few acted on
                  the page. The direction of the work was carried out by the owner Cage in collaboration
                  with Helen Livingstone and student Arthur Penn33, who also carried out the recording
                  of the work, but did not encounter the recorded material of the work.
                  The atrezzo and the scene were designed by Elaine and Willem De Kooning34 and

                  OA04_EXERCISES FOR THEATER (1949)
                   In 1949, under the direction of Charles Olson39, there was a program of theatrical performances in which
                   the theater and dance were mixed with different origins and epocas, such as the gray theater, Noh songs
                   and Hindu dance.
                   Olson, a poet and figure key in the final stage of the BMC, was interested in the experimentation of
                   theater and poetry as disciplines and indagaba in addition to possible performative reasons. His original
                   idea was to trade the BMC with a company that he worked in Washington for, in addition to his speeches,
                   “reinventing the theater”40 , however, due to the lack of financing, reformulated his course and offered a
                   representation llamada Wagadu, after Frobenius.




                   It was based on the popular cues of the Soninke tribe of Western Africa and was recorded in the African
                   Genesis (The Origin of African Cultures) by Leo Frobenius and Douglas Fox in 1937. The representation
                  was taken in collaboration with Pete Jennerjahn41 and students Dan Rice and Mark Hedden, combining
                   poetry, mime, music, color and dance. In it, Olson recited the song in a mode of song projected on slides,
                   accompanied by music by Pete Jennerjhan and with a scene designed by Dan Rice. These words are
                   based on “the new concept of theater”42 which is mixed, by one song, “the discourse, the sound and the
                   movement” and, by another, “proyecciones, the melody and the expression”, in the words of Olson43 .




                   The scene of this action was directed by Olson at the dinner table, a common scenario of activities of all
                   types, from assemblies to parties or varied representations.
                   A lugar abierto al suceso that allows it to transform into a function of the action to be deactivated. This
                   discovery is the precedent of his multidisciplinary exploration that began in The Glyph in 1951 and can
                   also be considered a precursor of Cage's happening in 1952.




                  OA05_THE KINGFISHERS (1950)
                   And so, in 1950, Olson created a dramatic representation of his poem The Kingfishers44, written a year
                   ago. The poem was a song about renewal and the possibility of change in the West. His protagonist, the
                   Martin fisherman, the kingfisher, was the symbol of renewal in various North American indigenous
                   cultures. The first verso of the poem will be a declaration of intentions, affirming that what no change is
                   the will to change:
